# 1. Changes made

Date: 27 Oct 2026. Branch `feat/authorship-humanize-2026-10-27`, commit `7a88568`. Source: `final.json` (fixer + adversarial verifier per job).


1035 claims fixed (733 from triaged questions, 302 found by fixers). 113 checked and already correct.

## Data values changed (with tests)

- ff-016-youth-worker: lib/data/minimum-wage-state/index.ts TAS_LAST_MINIMUM: {weekly 812.6, decided '22 December 2022', operative '1 August 2022'} -> {weekly 948, citation '[2026] TASICFB 2', decided '25 February 2026', operative 'the first full pay period starting on or after 1 August 2025'} (effective first full pay period on or after 1 Aug 2025; no test pins it)
- ff-016-youth-worker: lib/data/minimum-wage-state/index.ts NEW SA_STATE_MINIMUM_AWARD_WAGE = {weekly 1023, hourly 26.92, casualHourly 33.65}, effective from the first pay period starting on or after 1 July 2026 (SAET Local Government Employees Award cl 5.2.5.1); no test pins SA text
- ff-017-nt: lib/data/nursing-pay/nt.ts verifiedOn: "24 September 2026" -> "27 October 2026" (all scale values re-read against Schedule 3 Table 1; no rate changed)
- ff-017-nt: lib/data/nursing-pay/vic.ts instruments[0].nextIncrease: "first full pay period on or after 30 November 2026 (+1.06%)" -> "on and from 30 November 2026 (the agreement's column is headed 1.06%, but some steps rise by less, and Grade 2 Year 1 does not rise)" (clause 25.2(f)); no rate values changed
- ff-020-act: TAS_PUBLIC_HOLIDAYS.years[2027]: added 2027-12-25 'Christmas Day' (statewide). The 27 Dec entry is renamed from 'Christmas Day (observed)' (statewide) to 'Additional holiday (Christmas Day)' (additional). Basis: Statutory Holidays Act 2000 (Tas) s 4(i),(ia), in force since 2010.
- ff-020-act: TAS_PUBLIC_HOLIDAYS.years[2027]: added 2027-03-28 'Easter Sunday' (statewide). Effective 9 Oct 2026 (Statutory Holidays Amendment Act 2026, No. 18 of 2026, s 4 inserting s 4(da)).
- ff-020-act: TAS_PUBLIC_HOLIDAYS.standfirst 2027 count: 9 -> 11 (tied to the data by a test)
- ff-020-act: TAS_PUBLIC_HOLIDAYS.verifiedOn: 24 September 2026 -> 27 October 2026; Statutory Holidays Act 2000 (Tas) added to sources
- ff-020-act: seasonal.test.ts expectations: Christmas tas [2026-12-25, 2027-12-27] -> [2026-12-25, 2027-12-25, 2027-12-27]; Easter tas [2027-03-26, 2027-03-29] -> [2027-03-26, 2027-03-28, 2027-03-29]
- ff-021-aps-grades: qld.ts source qld-core-status verifiedOn: "28 August 2026" -> "27 October 2026" (forgov.qld.gov.au page fetched live 27 Oct 2026 and unchanged since its 5 Aug 2026 update)
- ff-024-hub: TAS_FIREFIGHTER_PAY.verifiedOn: "24 September 2026" -> "27 October 2026" (re-check of the TIC agreements list)
- ff-024-hub: VIC_FIREFIGHTER_PAY.verifiedOn: "24 September 2026" -> "27 October 2026" (re-read of the FRV page and the FWC B2023/771 page)
- ff-024-hub: VIC_FIREFIGHTER_PAY.ratesEffectiveFrom: "15 July 2026 (the date FRV published them; FRV does not say when they took effect)" -> "a date FRV does not state (its page, last updated 15 July 2026, lists them as current)"; no test asserts this value
- ff-027-index: lib/data/teacher-pay/vic.ts VIC_TEACHER_PAY.nextIncrease.date: 'pending publication of the Fair Work Commission's decision on the VGSA 2026' -> 'the first pay period on or after 1 November 2027' (VGSA 2026 Schedule 1 next column; agreement operative 23 October 2026 per [2026] FWCA 2836). No test checks this field.
- ff-028-wa: WA_TEACHER_PAY.verifiedOn: "28 August 2026" -> "27 October 2026" (re-check date; rates unchanged, effective 6 December 2025)
- ff-038-contractor-vs-employee-c: contractor-vs-employee-calculator-content.tsx EX_LEAVE: Math.round(100000 x 0.114) = 11,400 -> EX_AL + EX_PL from EMPLOYMENT (4/52 + 10/260) = 7,692 + 3,846 = 11,538 (NES entitlements, current; changes worked-example employee total 100,880 -> 101,018 and gap 21,600 -> 21,738)
- ff-038-contractor-vs-employee-c: contractor-vs-employee-calculator-content.tsx new derived EX_MATCH ($130,300 at FY2026-27 rates) and EX_MATCH_PCT (30%) replacing typed ranges $138,000–$145,000 / $135,000–$145,000 / 30–45% / 25–50%
- ff-038-contractor-vs-employee-c: contractor-vs-employee-calculator-content.tsx new MEDICARE_PCT (2%) and GST_THRESHOLD ($75,000, from lib/constants/gig-tax.ts GST_REGISTRATION_THRESHOLD) rendered in place of typed figures; no value change
- ff-066-construction-trades-pay: New in-file constant AATSP_FULL_TIME = { total: 2500, instalment: 625 } (apprenticeships.gov.au, AATSP current settings, continuing to 30 June 2029); replaces typed '$5,000–$10,000'
- ff-066-construction-trades-pay: New in-file constant KAP_APPRENTICE_FULL_TIME = { total: 10000, instalment: 2000, from: '1 July 2025' } (apprenticeships.gov.au Key Apprenticeship Program, commencements on or after 1 July 2025)
- ff-066-construction-trades-pay: Worked example base $40/hr -> CARPENTER headline row hourly $32.30 (rendered from data), result $360 -> $290.70 (computed)
- ff-086-new-job-checklist: Added local DASP_RATES { taxedElement 0.35, untaxedElement 0.45, whm 0.65 } in modules/guide/non-resident-tax-faqs.ts (ATO DASP page, last updated 21 Apr 2026). No lib/constants values changed, so no test updates.
- ff-090-overtime-penalty-rates-g: modules/guide/overtime-penalty-rates-guide-faqs.ts: new local FW_UNITS_LARGE_COMPANY = 1,500 penalty units (60 x 25) for companies with 15 or more employees, per the FWO litigation page (content updated 6 July 2026). No lib/constants values changed, so no test file was edited.

## Meta descriptions changed (fact wrong or figure now from code)

- `/payment-in-lieu-of-notice/`: the extra week needs over 45 years of age AND 2 years' service.
- `/public-holiday-pay/tas/`: 2027 count 9 -> 11 (Easter Sunday and Christmas Day added, Statutory Holidays Amendment Act 2026 (Tas)).
- `/age-pension-assets-test-calculator/`: figures now render from constants; visible change only '20 Sep' -> '20 September'.

## Internal links added
- `/final-pay-calculator/` -> `/long-service-leave-calculator/`
- `/retail-hospitality-pay-guide/` -> `/restaurant-award-rates/`
- `/news/award-wage-increase-2026-industries/` -> `/news/c13-classification-phase-out/`, `/news/junior-pay-rates-december-2026/`
